    // Return a random integer; this will be used as an 
    // identifier for all auctions on the page.
    function generateCorrelator() {
        // Use the Math.random function since
        // we do not need a cryptographically secure
        // number here
        var rand = Math.random();

    }
    var correlator1 = generateCorrelator();
    var correlator2 = generateCorrelator();
    if (correlator1 === correlator2) {

        // PRNG and thus is not a real user.
        window.mavenCorrelator = 0;

of Contents">Who Is Advertising in the 2024 Super Bowl?</a></li><li class="m-toc--item"><a class="m-toc--link" href="/living/super-bowl-commercial-cost#how-many-people-watch-the-super-bowl" onclick="return phoenixTrackClickEvent(this, event);" phx-track-id="Table of Contents">How Many People Watch the Super Bowl?</a></li></ul></aside><p>The&#xA0;<a href="https://parade.com/tv/super-bowl-2024">Super Bowl</a> is finally here; February 11 has officially arrived. And that means those iconic <a href="https://parade.com/tv/super-bowl-commercials-2023">Super Bowl&#xA0;advertisements</a> that so many of us love will also be aired before we know it. In fact, for some, the ads may be more fun to watch than even the game. It&apos;s always entertaining to see all of the <a href="https://parade.com/living/creativity-quotes">creativity</a> that goes into producing an ad that airs during the Super Bowl prime time. Advertisers will pay big bucks for a coveted time slot and they want to make it count. After all, when you are paying millions of dollars for just a 30-second time slot, you want to do everything possible to make it stand out.</p><aside class="m-in-content-ad-row l-inline the most-watched events on TV, businesses are willing to shell out plenty of cash to get their ad aired during the game. It makes sense that companies want to put their best representation forward when tens of millions of people are watching. And, to make ads memorable, it usually means we can count on plenty of humor. However, it&apos;s a no-joking matter when it comes to the cost of a Super Bowl ad. Even though the price seems unreal, it&apos;s definitely a fact and companies are willing to pay the cost.</p><aside class="m-in-content-ad-row l-inline mm-in-content-ad-row--in-content not-size-a Quarter(back)&#x2014;Here&apos;s How Much Super Bowl Tickets Cost in 2024</a></strong></p><h2 id="how-much-does-a-super-bowl-ad-cost-in-2023">How Much Does a Super Bowl Ad Cost in 2024?</h2><p>This year, CBS sold out all of their <a href="https://parade.com/983861/gwynnewatkins/super-bowl-commercials/">Super Bowl commercial</a> time slots, according to <a href="https://www.hollywoodreporter.com/business/business-news/2024-super-bowl-ads-sell-out-cbs-1235635484/" rel="nofollow noopener" onclick="return phoenixTrackClickEvent(this, event);" over a million dollars was in 1995 when it cost $1.15 million for the 30-second time slot. Each year, the cost continues to grow and the biggest jump was between 2021 and 2022 when the cost went from $5.5 million to $6.5 million.</p><p><strong>Related:&#xA0;<a href="https://parade.com/living/super-bowl-memes">Everybody Wins With These Must-See Super Bowl Memes</a></strong></p><h2 id="are-super-bowl-ads-worth-it">Are Super Bowl Ads Worth It?</h2><p>The&#xA0;<a href="https://twin-cities.umn.edu/news-events/impact-super-bowl-ads" rel="nofollow noopener" onclick="return phoenixTrackClickEvent(this, event);" target="_blank">University of Minnesota Carlson School of Management</a>&#xA0;published a study in 2022 on the effect Super Bowl ads have on business sales and growth. It was determined that Super Bowl advertisers may not see a huge boost in sales right away. However, there are immediate increases in online searches that help to spread brand recognition which could bring in later sales.</p><aside class="m-in-content-ad-row l-inline
